Testo inviato integralmente:

~~~text
Background illustration for a visual-novel dialogue scene in the original card game Botte Micidiali. One opaque landscape image, 1536 x 1024 pixels, aspect ratio 3:2. Use image 1 only as the approved art-direction reference: hand-inked European comic look, irregular dark contours, restrained painted shading, muted palette of walnut, tobacco brown, dusty bottle green, oxblood burgundy, muted petrol and worn ivory. Use image 2 only as the reference for the bar's materials and colours: dark scratched walnut wood and cracked oxblood vinyl. Do not copy anything else from either image.

The scene: the interior of a cramped, grimy Italian neighbourhood dive bar at night, seen at eye level from just inside the entrance, with nobody in it. In the central third of the width: the old bar counter with a worn wooden top, a few battered stools in front of it, shelves of dusty bottles with blank labels behind it and a cheap old espresso machine. Towards the sides: small tables with mismatched, cheaply repaired chairs and stained walls, as disposable side context. Warm, dim, dirty light. The lower quarter of the image is plain worn floor tiles with no important details, because a dialogue box will cover it, and the centre stays uncluttered, because a character portrait will stand in front of it. Everything important must stay inside the central third, because on phones only a narrow central strip is visible.

Avoid: any text, letters, numbers, signs, labels, logos, brands, signatures or watermarks; neon signs; people, faces, hands, silhouettes; playing cards, game interface, frames or borders; elegant, trendy, clean or renovated decor; photographic or 3D-rendered style; dominant yellow-and-black colours.
~~~

Edit the attached approved character sheet into a single half-body portrait of the same woman for a visual-novel dialogue scene. Framing: from the top of her head, with a small empty margin above it, down to her waist at the bottom edge; her eyes at about one quarter of the image height. Three-quarter view, turned toward the viewer. Background: fully transparent if supported; otherwise a perfectly flat, uniform magenta (#FF00FF) with no shadows, gradients or texture. Expression: pleased and excited, a crooked knowing grin, eyes still staring with deep dark circles, as if she has just figured something out.

Avoid: any other change to the character; mirroring; any text, letters, numbers, logos, brand names or recognizable branding of any real delivery company, signatures or watermarks; scenery or other characters; likeness of any real person or celebrity; photographic or 3D-rendered style.
~~~

## Composizioni e scontorni

Scontorni: non necessari; i quattro originali hanno canale alfa nativo. Composizioni realizzate con Pillow, script `%TEMP%\bm-fetta-composizioni.py`; sfondo ritagliato al centro, ritratti non specchiati, WebP qualità 90.

- [fetta-orizzontale-mara.webp](composizioni/fetta-orizzontale-mara.webp): 1366×768 px, WEBP, RGB, 302282 byte; SHA-256 `34a9cd18714f9bd85018467e043717f7058d0bfff41f32a00c7e335f6ae22a3a`
- [fetta-orizzontale-spazienzio.webp](composizioni/fetta-orizzontale-spazienzio.webp): 1366×768 px, WEBP, RGB, 309404 byte; SHA-256 `64e3fa11d15f91a551bc960543e4177499ef2705e0bc4cab4436d9be345c3c5c`
- [fetta-verticale-mara.webp](composizioni/fetta-verticale-mara.webp): 390×844 px, WEBP, RGB, 99978 byte; SHA-256 `a93ee38de2e5f69b99771252f57f53eec505a550958c2e2e056ea3cc61a0dda0`
- [fetta-verticale-spazienzio.webp](composizioni/fetta-verticale-spazienzio.webp): 390×844 px, WEBP, RGB, 105994 byte; SHA-256 `eb2cb948b897287b4d24b7a9eae1a00874c94f1d25a25d07e2834b7a180cea52`

Canvas 1024 x 1536 pixels, portrait 2:3. Framing: he is very broad, so frame him from a little further away than a normal half-body portrait: his whole shoulders and both arms, fists included, fit inside the canvas with a small empty margin on both sides; from the top of his head, with a small empty margin above it, down to his waist or a little below at the bottom edge; his eyes at about one quarter of the image height. Three-quarter view, turned toward the viewer. Background: fully transparent if supported; otherwise a perfectly flat, uniform magenta (#FF00FF) with no shadows, gradients or texture. Expression: explosive rage at its absolute peak, completely out of control: bellowing at the top of his lungs with his mouth wide open, eyes bulging, every vein on his head and neck swollen, face purple-red, leaning forward aggressively, both fists raised and shaking. Nothing comes out of his nose.

Avoid: any other change to the character; mirroring; arms or fists cut off by the edges of the canvas; steam, smoke, drips or lines coming out of his nose or head; any text, letters, numbers, logos, brands, signatures or watermarks; scenery or other characters; likeness of any real person or celebrity; photographic or 3D-rendered style.
~~~

### Composizioni del giro B e ritocco

Composizioni con Pillow, geometria e finestra della fetta; WebP qualità 90. Grappino senza finestra, a un quarto della larghezza e alto un sesto dello schermo, sul pavimento davanti al bancone. Alfa nativo, nessuno scontorno. Le immagini non conformi sono rese visibili per il giudizio del proprietario.

Background illustration for a visual-novel dialogue scene in the original card game Botte Micidiali. One opaque landscape image, 1536 x 1024 pixels, aspect ratio 3:2. Use image 1 only as the approved art-direction reference: hand-inked European comic look, irregular dark contours, restrained painted shading, muted palette of walnut, tobacco brown, dusty bottle green, oxblood burgundy, muted petrol and worn ivory. Use image 2, the approved interior of the same bar, only as the reference for drawing style, level of detail and mood, so that the two backgrounds belong together; do not copy its content.

The scene: the outside of a run-down Italian neighbourhood dive bar at night, seen at eye level from the street, nobody around. In the central third of the width: the entrance door with a worn frame and a small shop window beside it, warm dirty light inside, and above the door a plain rectangular lit sign panel with no lettering at all. To the left of the door, a stretch of stained wall where a bicycle could lean, left empty. Towards the sides: peeling plaster, a drainpipe, a rusty shutter, a dim street lamp, as disposable side context. The air of a place where you might catch tetanus. The lower quarter is plain worn pavement with no important details, because a dialogue box will cover it, and the centre stays uncluttered, because a character portrait will stand in front of it. Everything important must stay inside the central third.

Avoid: any text, letters, numbers, signs with writing, labels, logos, brands, signatures or watermarks; neon writing; people, faces, hands, silhouettes; playing cards, game interface, frames or borders; photographic or 3D-rendered style; dominant yellow-and-black colours.
~~~

The group stands at about the scale of people seen from a few metres away in a street, occupying roughly the central third of the width and the middle of the height, feet at about three quarters of the image height. Background: fully transparent if supported; otherwise a perfectly flat, uniform magenta (#FF00FF). Recognizable only by their outlines and poses: running away in a panic toward the right side, arms flailing, one looking back over the shoulder, one losing a shoe.

Avoid: faces, eyes, features, inner lines, colours, gradients or highlights inside the silhouettes; any text, letters, numbers, logos, brands, signatures or watermarks; scenery; photographic or 3D-rendered style.
~~~

### Composizioni del giro C

Composizioni con Pillow, geometria e finestra della fetta; WebP qualità 90. Sagome sovrapposte con la scala della tela, così resta visibile la difformità di inquadratura; bici ridimensionata e posta a sinistra della porta. Alfa nativo, nessuno scontorno.

## Secondo giro

21 novembre 2026. Per ciascun blocco un primo risultato e un solo nuovo tentativo, con il solo allegato della tabella verificato. I due scontorni successivi non sono usati e sono stati eliminati su indicazione della regia. Le misure della regia accettano i residui alfa 1–5 lontani dalla figura, invisibili e inferiori a quelli del ritratto approvato di Spazienzio (circa 1.800 pixel fino ad alfa 5).

## Campagna principale — giri E, F, G

Background illustration for a visual-novel dialogue scene in the original card game Botte Micidiali. One opaque landscape image, 1536 x 1024 pixels, aspect ratio 3:2. Use image 1 only as the approved art-direction reference: hand-inked European comic look, irregular dark contours, restrained painted shading, muted palette of walnut, tobacco brown, dusty bottle green, oxblood burgundy, muted petrol and worn ivory. Use image 2, the approved interior of the same trendy cocktail bar, only as the reference for drawing style, level of detail and its cleaner, cooler colours, so that the two backgrounds belong together; do not copy its content.

The scene: the outside of an expensive, pretentious disco pub at night, seen at eye level from the street, nobody around. In the central third of the width: a sleek entrance with a glossy black door, brass handles, a short red carpet, two chrome posts with a velvet rope between them, and above the door a plain rectangular lit sign panel with no lettering at all. Towards the sides: a dark glass front with cold indirect light, potted topiary plants, a polished pavement, as disposable side context. It must feel snobbish and overpriced, the exact opposite of a grimy neighbourhood dive bar, still drawn in the same hand-inked comic style. The lower quarter is plain pavement with no important details, because a dialogue box will cover it, and the centre stays uncluttered, because a character portrait will stand in front of it. Everything important must stay inside the central third.

Avoid: any text, letters, numbers, signs with writing, labels, logos, brands, signatures or watermarks; neon writing; people, faces, hands, silhouettes; playing cards, game interface, frames or borders; photographic or 3D-rendered style; dominant yellow-and-black colours.
